Expanded pre-card block to paste into each new row before shortening into the table:

```text
REQUEST/CUTOFF:
GAME-STATE: PREGAME / LIVE / LIKELY COMPLETE - local start, AEST start, request time.
FIXTURE/FORMAT:
RULESET LOCK: competition, governing body, rulebook/playing-conditions edition, competition variant, season phase.
SETTLEMENT CONVENTIONS: OT included / 90 minutes only / push number / DLS-sensitive / abandoned handling.
SETTLEMENT MATRIX: overtime, extra time, penalties/shootout, Super Over, DLS, reserve day, automatic runner, suspended-game continuation, official correction, protest/review correction.
OFFICIAL SOURCE ORDER: rulebook/playing conditions -> competition regulations -> official match centre/gamebook/scorecard -> verified stats/reporting.
SOURCES OPENED: source - timestamp/recency - access/status tag - fact used.
ELIGIBILITY / CLASSIFICATION CHECK: roster/team-sheet deadline, starters/XI/active list, suspensions, age/category/import clearance, concussion/HIA, tiebreak/classification if relevant.
AVAILABILITY VERDICT:
CONDITIONS/HIDDEN FACTORS: venue, weather, officials, travel/rest, motivation, surface/roof, tactical comments.
CRICKET SURFACE EVIDENCE (mandatory for cricket cards): ACTUAL PITCH REPORT: source/wording or UNKNOWN; CAPTAIN/TOSS COMMENTS: source/wording or none; LIVE SURFACE BEHAVIOUR: score/time trigger or not applicable. Do not use "road", "flat", "sticky", "green", "turner", or similar labels unless one of these three fields explicitly supports it.
STAT COVERAGE MATRIX: checked / MISSING / not relevant per stat; all four windows L5/L10/L15/L20 REQUIRED on decision-driving stats; missing L5/L10 caps confidence.
FORM DELTA TABLE: stat | L5 | L10 | L15 | L20 | SEASON | HIST(level) | d5 | d10 | dH | verdict (Sec 13.3-13.4).
FORM ANCHOR: window/subsample + reason (opponent-adjusted L5 / in-window regime change / Gate 6 default); REGIME FLAG sentence if |season-hist| > threshold.
PRESS SIGNAL: none found / not opened / S-class + speaker + role + date + source + verbatim quote -> direction hypothesis -> weight applied (Sec 14).
OFFICIALS: names + role + source + timestamp + access tag / NOT YET ASSIGNED (release timing) / SOURCE UNAVAILABLE (chain walked) (Sec 15).
SOURCE CONFIDENCE / DISAGREEMENT: decisive source confidence scores; source conflicts; confidence cap or none.
SIMILAR-CONDITION FORM: closest L5/L10/L20 sample by opponent/venue/weather/rest/role/ruleset; verdict or UNKNOWN.
ROLE / REPLACEMENT / BENCH: role changes, net replacement drop-off, bench/late-game strength, unresolved scenarios.
STYLE FIT: weather style fit, venue style fit, and opponent-fatigue exploitation path.
VOLATILITY / STAT STABILITY: stable vs noisy stats, floor/ceiling, regression, garbage-time adjustment.
MOTIVATION / NEWS / MARKET CONTEXT: exact incentive plus sourced news/public/market signal if allowed; otherwise NOT RELEVANT.
PREDICTION TRIGGERS: what would change the ranking before start or live.
PROJECTION FIRST: market -> projected band -> line distance -> coinflip flag if near line.
FRAMEWORK SWEEP: sports doc + v2/v3 logs + ledger + source registry + Poisson framework; closest lessons and conflicts.
MODEL TARGET / EXPOSURE:
POISSON BASELINE / DIAGNOSTICS:
CHALLENGER MODEL(S) / SELECTED MODEL + WHY:
TRAINING WINDOW / DECAY / INPUT CUTOFF:
SIMULATIONS / SEED:
MEAN / MEDIAN / SD / P10 / P25 / P75 / P90:
OVER / UNDER / PUSH / VOID / OUTCOME PROBABILITIES:
MODEL DISAGREEMENT / CALIBRATION STATUS:
PROBABILITY TABLE: each option -> probability bucket -> frequency-count base -> adjustments.
#1 STRESS TEST: mechanism, kill path, kill-path probability, downgrade if needed.
#4 SLOT TEST: probability <=30% target, failure paths, swap test, forced-bottom label if needed.
PASS/NO-EDGE: state PASS, LOW-EDGE FORCED RANKING, or ACTIVE EDGE.
FINAL RANKING: must match chat answer and this log exactly.
DISPUTE / OFFICIAL-CORRECTION CHECK: official final published; no unresolved protest, official-stat correction, suspended-game continuation, or replay correction.
SETTLEMENT UPDATE: final source, phase source, W-L-P, penalties/exemptions, calibration bucket update.
RETROSPECTIVE AUDIT CAUSE: wrong ruleset / wrong availability-eligibility / wrong settlement convention / source-status error / projection error / rank-slot calibration error / variance.
```
